Dearest Shareholders,                                                          尊敬的股東：

Embracing our heritage and forging a path to the future                        紮根文化傳統，開創未來之路
Risun stands tall with pride as we approach our 30th Anniversary               傲然屹立邁向旭陽三十週年

On behalf of the Board of Directors of China Risun Group Limited (“Risun”      本人榮幸代表中國旭陽集團有限公司（「旭陽」
or the “Company”), it is my privilege to present the annual report for the     或「本公司」）董事會，欣然呈報本公司及其附
Company and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”) for     屬公司（統稱「本集團」）截至2023年12月31日
the year ended on December 31, 2023 (the “Year” or “Reporting Period”).        止年度（「本年度」或「報告期間」）之年報。

As Risun approaches its 30th anniversary in 2025, we seize this                旭陽將於2025年邁向30週年，我們趁這隆
momentous occasion to reflect on the Company’s core strengths,                 重時刻，回顧成就公司輝煌歷史，貫穿未來
methodologies, ethos, and guiding principles that seamlessly connect           蓬勃發展的核心優勢、方針、理念及指導原
our rich past to a dynamic future. Risun has proudly held the position of      則。過去十幾年來，旭陽穩佔全球最大獨立
the world’s largest independent coke producer and supplier for over a          焦炭生產商及供應商寶座，在此穩固基礎
decade. Building upon this foundation, we have recently ascended to            上，我們最近榮升全球第二大己內酰胺生產
the position of the world’s second-largest producer of caprolactam and         商地位，並成為中國京津冀地區最大高純氫

ability to draw from its historical foundations to forge ahead, continually    之處，超越過往成就。戰略發展方面最為觸
seeking improvement and surpassing previous benchmarks. Our                    目的是產品年產能大幅上升、開拓更為多元

PROSPECTS                                                                     展望

Despite operating in a cyclical industry both in China and internationally,   儘管本集團在中國及國際上均於週期性行業
the Group’s business model and strategic regional presence in China are       營運，但本集團設計在中國的商業模式及戰
designed to mitigate cyclical impacts through the following approaches:       略區域佈局為通過以下方針減緩週期性影響：

1.    by balancing the production of both coke and refined chemical           1.   平衡具有不同需求週期的焦炭及精細化
      products, which experience varying demand cycles, we are able to             工產品的生產，使我們能夠利用部分精
      leverage the robust demand for certain refined chemicals to sustain          細化工產品的旺盛需求保持業務增長；
      our business growth;

2.    capitalizing on its leading position and extensive experience in coke   2.   本集團憑藉於焦炭生產方面的領先地位
      production, the Group has successfully expanded its business by              及豐厚經驗，通過併購與運營管理服務
      striking a strategic balance between mergers and acquisitions and            達致戰略平衡來拓展業務。此為協調
      operational management services. This approach harmonizes the                「輕資產」與「資本密集」投資思維的方
      “asset-light” and “capital-intensive” investment philosophies;               針；

3.    by venturing into the development of new materials and new energy       3.   本公司藉投入新材料、新能源開發，將
      sources, the Company is diversifying beyond its traditional focus on         傳統焦炭與精細化工的焦點多元化。此
      coke and refined chemicals. This expansion creates a synergistic             拓展方式能在成立多年的業務線與新興
      balance between the established business lines and the emerging              的純氫領域之間建立協同平衡；及
      purified hydrogen sector; and

4.    bolstering the Group’s competitive edge by integrating advanced         4.   通過將自動化及信息化技術融入本集團
      automation and information technologies into its operations.                 營運，增強其競爭優勢。

The Group is committed to smoothing out the cyclical fluctuations inherent    本集團以理念及戰略目標為主導，致力解決
in our industry, guided by our vision and strategic objectives. We are        行業週期性波動問題。我們堅決業務發展配
resolved to align our business development with the overarching trends of     合中國國家宏觀經濟大趨勢，深信中國經濟
China’s national macroeconomy, which we believe will continue to thrive.      將繼續蓬勃發展。本集團承擔高標準的環境
Embracing a high standard of environmental and social responsibility,         社會責任，積極協調業務增長及國家發展目
the Group is proactive in harmonizing its business growth with the            標。旭陽堅持秉承以客戶為中心的經營理
nation’s development goals. Risun steadfastly adheres to a customer-          念，致力為客戶創造價值。我們務求實現業
centric philosophy, dedicated to creating value for our clients. We aim for   務共贏發展，推動焦炭、新材料及新能源行
mutually beneficial business progress and to foster innovation within the     業的創新。
coke, new materials, and new energy sectors.

     主席報告（續）


     Looking forward in 2024, the Group will continue to increase the market         展望2024年，本集團將繼續透過拓展營運管
     share of all key products by expanding the operation management                 理及併購，增加所有主要產品的市場份額。
     together with merger and acquisition. The Group also plans to strengthen        本集團亦計劃強化焦炭及精細化工產品生產
     the production line of coke and refined chemicals and further refining into     線，深入推進如己內酰胺(CPL)及氫基產品等
     new materials and new energy such as caprolactam (CPL) and hydrogen-            新材料、新能源的精細化。我們將以符合當
     based products. Our primary focus is to expand the production of our            前中國及全球經濟環境為前題，集中擴大生
     existing product mix and to introduce new refined chemical products that        產現有產品組合及推出新的精細化工產品。
     align with the current economic climate both in China and globally.

     DIVIDEND                                                                        股息

     Reflecting the operating performance and considering the business               根據經營業績達標程度及經考慮業務展望，
     outlook, the Board proposes a final dividend for the year of approximately      董事會就本年度建議派發末期股息約人民幣
     RMB53 million. This, combined with the interim dividend for 2023, brings        53百萬元。經合計2023年中期股息，本年度
     the total dividend for the Year to approximately RMB270 million. This           股息總額約為人民幣270百萬元，相當於本集
     represents a minimum of 30% of the Group’s net distributable profits for        團年度可分配淨利潤約人民幣861百萬元之最
     the year, which amount to approximately RMB861 million, as a gesture of         少30%，作為對本公司股東的回報。
     appreciation to the Company’s shareholders.

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                               業務回顧

The Group’s vertically integrated business model and its experience of        本集團縱向一體化的業務模式及在焦炭化工
more than 28 years in the coke industry production chain enables the          業生產鏈逾28年的經驗使本集團能夠拓展下
Group to widen the downstream refined chemicals industry. Currently,          游精細化工產品市場。目前，本集團有四個
the Group has four business segments ranging from coke and coking             業務分部，包括焦炭及焦化產品生產、精細
chemicals manufacturing, refined chemicals (including hydrogen-energy         化工產品（包括氫能產品）生產、運營管理服
products) manufacturing, operation management services and trading.           務及貿易。

The Group engaged in its first operation management service in 2014 and       本集團於2014年首次受委提供運營管理服
has been actively developing up to the Year for a decade in operation         務，截至本年度在過去十年一直積極發展運
management business. During the Reporting Period and up to the date           營管理業務。於報告期內及直至本報告日
of this report, the Group entered into four new operation management          期，本集團新訂了四份運營管理協議，進一
agreements to further expand the Group’s business presence in coke and        步拓展本集團於中國山西省、山東省及吉林
coking chemicals, including aniline and benzene hydrogenation refined         省的焦炭及焦化產品的業務佈局，包括苯胺
chemicals sectors, in Shanxi, Shandong and Jilin Province, China.             及苯加氫精細化工產品領域。

Relationship with Suppliers                                                  與供應商的關係

The principal raw material used for the coke and coking chemical             焦炭及焦化產品生產所用的主要原材料為焦
production is coking coal. The Group mainly sources the coking coal from     煤。本集團主要向外部供應商採購焦煤。本
external suppliers. The major types of coking coal the Group purchased       集團所採購的主要焦煤種類包括主焦煤、肥
included hard coking coal, fat coal, gas coal, 1/3 coking coal and lean      煤、氣煤、1/3焦煤及瘦煤。本集團大部分
coal. The Group purchased a large proportion of the coking coal from         焦煤乃採購自中國山西省及河北省的煤炭供
suppliers in Shanxi Province and Hebei Province, China. If overseas          應商。倘海外焦煤較國內焦煤價格更具競爭
coking coal is more competitively priced than domestic coking coal,          力，本集團將向蒙古及澳洲等國家採購焦煤。
the Group will source coking coal from countries such as Mongolia and
Australia.

For the production of the refined chemicals, the principal raw materials     就精細化工產品生產而言，主要原材料為焦
are coking chemicals, which include crude benzene, coal tar and coke         化產品，包括粗苯、煤焦油及焦爐煤氣。焦
oven gas. Coking chemicals are by-products in the coking process. The        化產品是煉焦過程中的副產品。本集團主要
Group mainly sources such raw materials externally from the suppliers of     透過本集團的供應商從外部採購有關原材
the Group, but also sources a portion of such raw materials by utilizing     料，但亦從定州生產園區及呼和浩特生產園
by-products from the coke production facilities at Dingzhou Production       區的焦炭生產設施及邢台生產園區的中煤旭
Base and Huhhot Production Base and from CNC Risun Coking at Xingtai         陽焦化採購部分原材料，以善用副產品。
Production Base.

with key suppliers. These supply arrangements specify the indicative         排。此等供應安排規定本集團計劃於相關期

relevant period and are subject to separate purchase orders to confirm       單的約束以確認交貨。供應商通常賦予本集
delivery. The suppliers typically granted the Group credit terms of 30 to    團30至90天的信貸期。根據此等安排，採購
90 days. Pursuant to these arrangements, the procurement department          部門根據生產部門製定的生產計劃從供應商
purchases the required raw materials from suppliers in accordance with       處採購所需原材料。一般而言，購買價為基
production plans prepared by the production department. Generally,           於原材料交付時的現行市場價格。本集團主
purchase prices are based on prevailing market prices at the time of         要根據客戶訂單的定期檢討制定生產計劃，
delivery of the raw materials. The Group makes and implements our raw        並按計劃制定及實施原材料採購計劃，以減
material procurement plans in line with the production plans which are       少本集團所面臨的原材料市場價格波動風

The internal market analysis reports generated by the market department
also provide us with a basis for determining procurement prices.

Purpose                                                                      目的

The purpose of the Share Option Scheme is to provide incentives to           購股權計劃旨在向參與者（定義見下段）提供
Participants (as defined in the following paragraph) to contribute to the    鼓勵以向本公司作出貢獻並激勵彼等作出更
Company and motivate them to higher levels of performance, and to            佳表現，以及使本公司能夠招聘高質素僱員
enable the Company to recruit high caliber employees and attract or          並吸引或挽留對本集團具價值的人力資源。
retain human resources that are valuable to the Group.

Who May Join                                                                 可參與人士

The Board may, at its absolute discretion, offer options to subscribe for     董事會可全權酌情根據購股權計劃所載的條
such number of Shares in accordance with the terms set out in the Share       款向本集團任何成員公司的任何僱員、執行
Option Scheme (“Options”) to any employee, executive director or non-         董事或非執行董事（包括獨立非執行董事）
executive director (including independent non-executive director) of any     （「參與者」）提呈可認購有關數目的股份的購
member of the Group (the “Participants”).                                     股權（「購股權」）。

all options to be granted under the Share Option Scheme (the “Maximum        股權計劃將予授出的所有購股權而可能發行
Number of Shares”) and any other Share Option Scheme of the Group            的最高股份數目（「最高股份數目」），合共不
shall not in aggregate exceed 10% of the total number of Shares in issue     得超過緊隨全球發售完成後的已發行股份總
immediately upon completion of the Global Offering, however, that,           數的10%，惟在遵守上市規則的前提下：
subject to compliance with the Listing Rules:

NOMINATION OF DIRECTORS AND BOARD                                            董事提名及董事會多元化政策
DIVERSITY POLICY

In order to enhance the quality of the performance of the Board and          為了提高董事會的業績質量並支持實現本公
to support the attainment of the Company’s strategic objectives and          司的策略性目標及可持續發展，我們已採納
sustainable development, we have adopted a board diversity policy            董事會多元化政策（「董事會多元化政策」）。
(the “Board Diversity Policy”). Pursuant to the Board Diversity Policy, we   根據董事會多元化政策，我們在選擇董事會
seek to achieve Board diversity through the consideration of a number        人選時考慮多個因素，包括但不限於性別、
of factors when selecting candidates to the Board, including but not         技能、年齡、專業經驗、知識、文化及教育
limited to gender, skills, age, professional experience, knowledge,          背景、種族及服務年期，以求實現董事會多
cultural and education background, ethnicity and length of service.          元化。最終將按選定候選人的長處及將為董
Appointments will ultimately be based on merit and the contributions the     事會所作貢獻而作委任。董事會相信以唯才
selected candidates will bring to the Board. The Board believes that such    是用之原則委任董事將最有利於本公司繼續
merit-based appointments will best enable the Company to service our         為其股東以至其他持份者服務。
Shareholders and other stakeholders.

The Nomination Committee of the Board is responsible for reviewing           董事會提名委員會負責審閱董事會架構、成
the structure, number of members, and composition of the Board of            員人數及組成。此外，委員會根據本公司的
Directors. In addition, the Committee makes recommendations to the           策略計劃、業務營運、資產規模及股權架構
Board relating to the size and structure of the Board of Directors based     向董事會就有關董事會規模及架構提供建
on the Company’s strategic plans, business operations, asset size and        議。委員會亦會討論及審查相關董事的甄選
shareholding structure. The Committee also discusses and reviews the         標準、提名及委任程序，並向董事會提供建

              3.2     Impacts on application of Amendments to IAS 1 and                       3.2   應用國際會計準則第1號及國
                      IFRS Practice Statement 2 Disclosure of Accounting                            際財務報告準則實務報告第2
                      Policies                                                                      號「會計政策之披露」
                                                                                                             （ 修訂本）
                                                                                                    之影響

                      The Group has applied the amendments for the first time in                    本集團於本年度首次應用該等修
                      the current year. IAS 1 Presentation of Financial Statements                  訂。國際會計準則第1號「呈列財
                      is amended to replace all instances of the term “significant                  務報表」予以修訂，以「重大會計
                      accounting policies” with “material accounting policy                         政策資料」取代「主要會計政策」
                      information”. Accounting policy information is material if, when              一詞的所有指述。倘連同實體財
                      considered together with other information included in an                     務報表內其他資料一併考慮，會
                      entity’s financial statements, it can reasonably be expected to               計政策資料可以合理預期會影響
                      influence decisions that the primary users of general purpose                 通用財務報表的主要使用者根據
                      financial statements make on the basis of those financial                     該等財務報表所作出的決定，則
                      statements.                                                                   該會計政策資料屬重大。

                      The amendments also clarify that accounting policy                            該等修訂亦澄清即使涉及款項並
                      information may be material because of the nature of the                      不重大，但基於相關交易性質、
                      related transactions, other events or conditions, even if the                 其他事項或情況，會計政策資料
                      amounts are immaterial. However, not all accounting policy                    仍可屬重大。然而，並非所有與
                      information relating to material transactions, other events or                重大交易、其他事項或情況有關
                      conditions is itself material. If an entity chooses to disclose               的會計政策資料本身即屬重大。
                      immaterial accounting policy information, such information                    倘一間實體選擇披露非重大會計
                      must not obscure material accounting policy information.                      政策資料，則有關資料不得掩蓋
                                                                                                    重大會計政策資料。

                      IFRS Practice Statement 2 Making Materiality Judgements (the                  國際財務報告準則實務報告第2
                      “Practice Statement”) is also amended to illustrate how an                    「作出重大性判斷」
                                                                                                    號         （「實務報
                      entity applies the “four-step materiality process” to accounting              告」）亦予以修訂，以說明一間實
                      policy disclosures and to judge whether information about                     體如何將「四步法評估重大性流
                      an accounting policy is material to its financial statements.                 程」應用於會計政策披露及判斷
                      Guidance and examples are added to the Practice Statement.                    有關一項會計政策的資料對其財
                                                                                                    務報表是否屬重大。實務報告已
                                                                                                    增加指導意見及實例。

              (CONTINUED)

              Basis of consolidation                                                          綜合基準

              The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                 綜合財務報表包含本公司及本公司控制
              statements of the Company and entities controlled by the Company                的實體及其附屬公司的財務報表。本公
              and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved where the Company:                    司於以下情況擁有控制權：

              •       has power over the investee;                                            •   可對投資對象行使權力；

              •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                 •   因參與投資對象之營運而承受所
                      involvement with the investee; and                                          得浮動回報之風險或享有回報之
                                                                                                  權利；及

              •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                 •   能行使權力影響回報。

              The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts            倘有事實及情況顯示上述三個控制因素
              and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more                其中一個或以上有變，本集團會重新評
              of the three elements of control listed above.                                  估其是否控制投資對象。

              Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains                     本集團在取得附屬公司的控制權時開始
              control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses                     將該附屬公司綜合入賬，並於失去該附
              control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses                    屬公司的控制權時終止綜合入賬。具體
              of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are                     而言，於年內所收購或出售的附屬公司
              included in the consolidated statements of profit or loss and other             的收入及開支由本集團取得控制權當日
              comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control until                開始計入綜合損益及其他全面收益表，
              the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                       直至本集團不再控制該附屬公司當日為
                                                                                              止。

              Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income are                  損益及其他全面收益各項目乃歸屬予本
              attributed to the owners of the Group and to the non-controlling                集團擁有人及非控股權益。附屬公司的
              interests. Total comprehensive income of subsidiaries is attributed             全面收益總額乃歸屬予本公司擁有人及
              to the owners of the Company and to the non-controlling interests               非控股權益，即使此舉導致非控股權益
              even if this results in the non-controlling interests having a deficit          出現虧絀結餘。
              balance.

              Where necessary, adjustments are made to the financial statements               如有需要，將對附屬公司的財務報表作
              of subsidiaries to bring their accounting policies in line with the             出調整，使其會計政策與本集團的會計
              Group’s accounting policies.                                                    政策相符。

              All intra-group assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses and            所有與本集團成員公司之間的交易有關
              cash flows relating to transactions between members of the Group                的集團內公司間資產及負債、權益、收
              are eliminated in full on consolidation.                                        入、開支以及現金流量均於綜合時全數
                                                                                              抵銷。

              Non-controlling interests in subsidiaries are presented separately              於附屬公司的非控股權益與本集團於當
              from the Group’s equity therein, which represent present ownership              中的權益分開呈列，指現時所有者權益
              interests entitling their holders to a proportionate share of net assets        且賦予持有人權利於清盤時按比例分佔
              of the relevant subsidiaries upon liquidation.                                  相關附屬公司淨資產。

     Business combinations                                                          業務合併

     A business is an integrated set of activities and assets which                 業務指一組綜合活動及資產，包括投入
     includes an input and a substantive process that together                      及實質過程，且兩者對創造產出的能力
     significantly contribute to the ability to create outputs. The acquired        有重大貢獻。倘收購過程對繼續生產產
     processes are considered substantive if they are critical to the               出的能力至關重要，則該收購流程被視
     ability to continue producing outputs, including an organised                  為具實質性，包括具備執行相關過程所
     workforce with the necessary skills, knowledge, or experience to               必需的技能、知識或經驗的組織勞動
     perform the related processes or they significantly contribute to the          力，或對持續生產產出的能力有重大貢
     ability to continue producing outputs and are considered unique                獻，且均被認為屬獨特或稀缺，或彼等
     or scarce or cannot be replaced without significant cost, effort, or           在無重大成本、努力或持續生產產出能
     delay in the ability to continue producing outputs.                            力出現延遲的情況下不可取代。

     Acquisitions of businesses are accounted for using the acquisition             收購業務以收購法入賬。於業務合併中
     method. The consideration transferred in a business combination                所轉讓之代價按公允值計量，即本集團
     is measured at fair value, which is calculated as the sum of the               為換取被收購方控制權而轉收的資產、
     acquisition-date fair values of the assets transferred by the Group,           本集團對被收購方前擁有人產生的負債
     liabilities incurred by the Group to the former owners of the acquiree         及本集團發行的權益工具於收購日期的
     and the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for control           公允值總和計算。收購相關成本一般於
     of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally recognized            產生時於損益內確認。
     in profit or loss as incurred.

     controlling interests in the acquiree and the fair value of the                  額及收購方先前在被收購方持有的權益

     excess is recognized immediately in profit or loss as a bargain                  即作為廉價收購收益計入損益。
     purchase gain.

     Non-controlling interests that are present ownership interests                   非控股權益屬現時擁有權權益且於清盤
     and entitle their holders to a proportionate share of the relevant               時讓持有人有權按比例分佔相關附屬公

     or at fair value. The choice of measurement basis is made on a                   易選擇。
     transaction-by-transaction basis.

     When the consideration transferred by the Group in a business                    倘本集團於業務合併時轉讓之代價包含
     combination includes a contingent consideration arrangement, the                 或然代價安排，或然代價將按其收購日

     adjusted retrospectively. Measurement period adjustments are                     （不超出收購日期起計一年）因取得於收

     the “measurement period” (which cannot exceed one year from the                  而作出之調整。
     acquisition date) about facts and circumstances that existed at the
     acquisition date.

              larger than an operating segment.

              Interests in associates and joint ventures                                   於聯營公司及合營企業的權益

              An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant               聯營公司是指本集團對其有重大影響力
              influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the          的實體。重大影響力指有權參與被投資
              financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is not          方的財務及經營政策決定但非控制或共
              control or joint control over those policies.                                同控制該等政策。

              A joint venture is a joint arrangement whereby the parties that have         合營企業為一種合營安排，據此，對安
              joint control of the arrangement have rights to the net assets of the        排擁有共同控制權的訂約方對合營安排
              joint arrangement. Joint control is the contractually agreed sharing         的淨資產享有權利。共同控制權指按合
              of control of an arrangement, which exists only when decisions               約協定共享對安排的控制權，僅於與相
              about the relevant activities require unanimous consent of the               關活動有關的決策須獲共享控制權的訂
              parties sharing control.                                                     約方一致同意時存在。

              Leases                                                                        租賃

              Definition of a lease                                                         租賃的定義

              A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the right         倘合約給予權利在一段時間內使用已識
              to control the use of an identified asset for a period of time in             別資產以換取代價，則該合約為或包含
              exchange for consideration.                                                   租賃。

              For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date of initial        就於首次應用國際財務報告準則第16
              application of IFRS 16 or arising from business combinations, the             號當日或之後簽訂或修訂的或因業務合
              Group assesses whether a contract is or contains a lease based on             併而產生的合約而言，本集團於開始或
              the definition under IFRS 16 at inception or modification date. Such          修訂日期根據於國際財務報告準則第
              contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and conditions of            16號項下的定義評估合約是否為或包
              the contract are subsequently changed.                                        含租賃。該合約將不會被重新評估，除
                                                                                            非該合約中的條款與條件隨後被改動。

              The Group as a lessee                                                         本集團作為承租人

              Allocation of consideration to components of a contract                       分配至合約組成部分的代價

              For a contract that contains a lease component and one or more                就包含租賃組成部分以及一項或多項額
              additional lease or non-lease components, the Group allocates the             外租賃或非租賃組成部分的合約而言，
              consideration in the contract to each lease component on the basis            本集團根據租賃組成部分的相關獨立價
              of the relative stand-alone price of the lease component and the              格及非租賃組成部分的獨立價格總額於
              aggregate stand-alone price of the non-lease components.                      合約分配代價至各租賃組成部分。

              The Group applies practical expedient not to separate non-lease               本集團亦採用實際權宜方法，不將非租
              components from lease component, and instead account for the                  賃組成部分與租賃組成部分分開，反而
              lease component and any associated non-lease components as a                  將租賃組成部分及任何相關非租賃組成
              single lease component.                                                       部分入賬列為單一租賃組成部分。

     Classification and measurement of leases                                       租賃的分類及計量

     Leases for which the Group is a lessor are classified as finance               本集團作為出租人的租賃分類為融資租
     or operating leases. Whenever the terms of the lease transfer                  賃或經營租賃。當租賃的條款實質上將
     substantially all the risks and rewards incidental to ownership of an          與相關資產所有權相關的所有風險及報
     underlying asset to the lessee, the contract is classified as a finance        酬轉讓給承租人時，該項合約被歸類為
     lease. All other leases are classified as operating leases.                    融資租賃。所有其他租賃應歸類為經營
                                                                                    租賃。

     Rental income from operating lease is recognized in profit or loss             經營租賃的租金收入於相關租約租期內
     on a straight-line basis over the term of the relevant lease. Initial          以直線法於損益中確認。磋商及安排經
     direct costs incurred in negotiating and arranging an operating                營租約產生的初始直接成本，計入出租
     lease are added to the carrying amount of the leased asset, and                資產的賬面值，該等成本以直線法在租
     such costs are recognized as an expense on a straight-line basis               賃年期確認為開支。將根據指數或比率
     over the lease term. Variable lease payments for operating leases              估算經營租賃的可變租賃付款，並將其
     that depend on an index or a rate are estimated and included in the            計入於租賃年期內將以直線法確認的租
     total lease payments to be recognized on a straight-line basis over            賃付款總額中。不取決於指數或比率的
     the lease term. Variable lease payments that do not depend on an               可變租賃付款於產生時確認為收入。
     index or a rate are recognized as income when they arise.

     Borrowing costs                                                                借款成本

     Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition of qualifying         直接歸屬於收購合資格資產（指必須經

     ready for their intended use or sale.                                          用途或銷售為止。

     Any specific borrowing that remain outstanding after the related               任何於有關資產可作其擬定用途或出售
     asset is ready for its intended use or sale is included in the                 後仍未償還的特定借款，計入一般性借
     general borrowing pool for calculation of capitalization rate on               款組合，以計算一般性借款的資本化

     capitalization.

     Current and deferred tax are recognized in profit or loss, except                即期及遞延稅項於損益中確認，惟與於
     when it relates to items that are recognized in other comprehensive              其他全面收益或直接於權益確認的項目
     income or directly in equity, in which case, the current and deferred            相關者除外，於該情況下，即期及遞延
     tax are also recognized in other comprehensive income or directly                稅項亦分別於其他全面收益或直接於權
     in equity respectively. Where current tax or deferred tax arises from            益確認。倘因對業務合併進行初始會計
     the initial accounting for a business combination, the tax effect is             處理而產生即期稅項或遞延稅項，則稅
     included in the accounting for the business combination.                         務影響計入業務合併的會計處理內。

     In assessing any uncertainty over income tax treatments, the Group               於評估所得稅處理的任何不確定性時，
     considers whether it is probable that the relevant tax authority will            本集團會考慮相關稅務部門會否接納個
     accept the uncertain tax treatment used, or proposed to be used by               別集團實體分別填報所得稅時使用的或
     individual group entities in their income tax filings. If it is probable,        建議使用的不確定稅務處理之可能性。
     the current and deferred taxes are determined consistently with the              如果接納，即期及遞延稅項會與填報所
     tax treatment in the income tax filings. If it is not probable that the          得稅時之稅務處理一致地釐定。如果有
     relevant taxation authority will accept an uncertain tax treatment, the          關稅務部門不可能接納不確定的稅務處
     effect of each uncertainty is reflected by using either the most likely          理，每個不確定性之影響利用最大可能
     amount or the expected value.                                                    金額或預期值反映。

     Properties for/under development which are intended to be sold                  於發展完成後擬作出售之待發展╱發展
     upon completion of development and properties for sale are                      中物業及待售物業均分類為流動資產。
     classified as current assets. Except for the leasehold land element             除租賃土地部分按照使用權資產之會計
     which is measured at cost model in accordance with the accounting               政策以成本模式計量外，待發展╱發展
     policies of right-of-use assets, properties for/under development/              中物業╱待售物業均以成本及可變現淨
     properties for sale are carried at the lower of cost and net realisable         值兩者之較低者列賬。成本按特定識別
     value. Cost is determined on a specific identification basis including          基準釐定，包括分配所產生之有關開發
     allocation of the related development expenditure incurred and                  開支及（倘適用）資本化借貸成本。可變
     where appropriate, borrowing costs capitalised. Net realisable                  現淨值指物業估計售價減完成之估計成
     value represents the estimated selling price for the properties less            本及作出銷售之必要成本。進行銷售之
     estimated cost to completion and costs necessary to make the                    所需成本包括銷售直接應佔之增量成本
     sales. Costs necessary to make the sale include incremental costs               及本集團為進行銷售而必須涉及之非增
     directly attributable to the sale and non-incremental costs which the           量成本。
     Group must incur to make the sale.

     Properties for/under development for sale are transferred to                    待發展╱發展中待售物業於完成後轉撥
     properties for sale upon completion.                                            至待售物業。

     Provisions                                                                      撥備

     Provisions are recognized when the Group has a present obligation               倘本集團因過往事件須承擔現時責任
     (legal or constructive) as a result of a past event, it is probable that        （法定或推定），則本集團須履行該責
     the Group will be required to settle that obligation, and a reliable            任，且該責任所涉金額能夠可靠估計，
     estimate can be made of the amount of the obligation.                           則確認撥備。

     The amount recognized as a provision is the best estimate of the                經考慮與責任有關之風險及不確定因素
     consideration required to settle the present obligation at the end of           後，確認為撥備之金額為於報告期末清
     the reporting period, taking into account the risks and uncertainties           償現時責任所需代價之最佳估計。倘撥
     surrounding the obligation. When a provision is measured using the              備乃以估計用作清償現時責任之現金流
     cash flows estimated to settle the present obligation, its carrying             量計量，則其賬面值為該等現金流量之
     amount is the present value of those cash flows (where the effect of            現值（倘貨幣時值影響屬重大）。
     the time value of money is material).

              Contingent liabilities                                                           或然負債

              A contingent liability is a present obligation arising from past events          或然負債指因已發生的事件而產生的現
              but is not recognized because it is not probable that an outflow of              有責任，但由於可能不需要流出具有經
              resources embodying economic benefits will be required to settle                 濟利益的資源以履行責任，或責任金額
              the obligation or the amount of the obligation cannot be measured                未能充分可靠地計量，故不予確認。
              with sufficient reliability.

              Where the Group is jointly and severally liable for an obligation, the           倘本集團對一項義務承擔連帶及個別責
              part of the obligation that is expected to be met by other parties               任，則該責任中預期由其他方履行的部
              is treated as a contingent liability and it is not recognized in the             分被視為或然負債，且不在綜合財務報
              consolidated financial statements.                                               表中確認。

              The Group assesses continually to determine whether an outflow of                本集團持續進行評估，以確定具有經濟
              resources embodying economic benefits has become probable. If                    利益的資源是否可能流出。倘先前作為
              it becomes probable that an outflow of future economic benefits will             或然負債處理的項目將可能需要未來經
              be required for an item previously dealt with as a contingent liability,         濟利益流出，則在發生概率變動的報告
              a provision is recognized in the consolidated financial statements               期內於綜合財務報表中確認撥備，惟極
              in the reporting period in which the change in probability occurs,               少數情況下無法進行可靠估計的情況除
              except in the extremely rare circumstances where no reliable                     外。
              estimate can be made.

              way purchases or sales are purchases or sales of financial assets                規管或慣例確立之時限內交付資產的金
              that require delivery of assets within the time frame established by             融資產購買或出售。
              regulation or convention in the market place.

              fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts with              量，惟客戶合約產生的貿易應收款項初
              customers which are initially measured in accordance with IFRS 15                步根據國際財務報告準則第15號「客戶
              Revenue from Contracts with Customers. Transaction costs that are                合約收入」計量。收購或發行金融資產
              directly attributable to the acquisition or issue of financial assets            及金融負債（除按公允值計入損益的金

              liabilities at FVTPL) are added to or deducted from the fair value of            易成本於初步確認時計入金融資產或金

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                    實際利率法是一種計算相關期間內金融
     amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and of               資產或金融負債的攤銷成本以及分配利
     allocating interest income and interest expense over the relevant               息收入和利息開支的方法。實際利率為
     period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly discounts          於金融資產或金融負債的預期存續期或
     estimated future cash receipts and payments (including all fees and             （如適用）更短期間內能夠精確計算預計
     points paid or received that form an integral part of the effective             未來現金收入及款項（包括構成實際利
     interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums or discounts)               率不可或缺部分之一切已付或已收費用
     through the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,        及點數、交易成本及其他溢價或折讓）
     or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying amount             貼現至初步確認時賬面淨值的利率。
     on initial recognition.

              Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible              全期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具預期年
              default events over the expected life of the relevant instrument.               期內發生的所有可能違約事件所導致的
              In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the portion of                 預期信貸虧損。與此相對，12個月預
              lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events that are            期信貸虧損（「12個月預期信貸虧損」）
              possible within 12 months after the end of each reporting period.               指預期於各報告期末後12個月內可能
              Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss                發生的違約事件所導致的部分全期預期
              experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,              信貸虧損。評估根據本集團過往信貸虧
              general economic conditions and an assessment of both the                       損經驗進行，並根據應收賬款特定因

              a provision matrix with appropriate groupings except that significant           損使用具有適當分組的撥備矩陣進行集
              balances are assessed individually.                                             體評估（重大結餘個別評估除外）。

              equal to 12m ECL, unless there has been a significant increase                  撥備等於12個月預期信貸虧損，除非

     Significant increase in credit risk                                             信貸風險大幅增加

     In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly                於評估信貸風險是否自初始確認以來大
     since initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a default             幅增加時，本集團比較金融工具於各報
     occurring on the financial instrument as at the end of each reporting           告期末出現違約的風險與該金融工具於
     period with the risk if a default occurring on the financial instrument         初始確認日期出現違約的風險。作此評
     as at the date of initial recognition. In making this assessment, the           估時，本集團會考慮合理及有理據的定
     Group considers both quantitative and qualitative information that              量及定性資料，包括歷史經驗及毋須花
     is reasonable and supportable, including historical experience and              費不必要成本或精力即可獲得的前瞻性
     forward-looking information that is available without undue cost or             資料。
     effort.

     In particular, the following information is taken into account when             尤其是，評估信貸風險是否大幅增加時
     assessing whether credit risk has increased significantly:                      會考慮下列信息：

     •     an actual or expected significant deterioration in the financial          •    金融工具外部（如有）或內部信貸
           instrument’s external (if available) or internal credit rating;                評級的實際或預期重大惡化；

     •     significant deterioration in external market indicators of credit         •    信貸風險的外界市場指標的重大
           risk, e.g. a significant increase in the credit spread, the credit             惡化，例如信貸息差大幅增加、
           default swap prices for the debtor;                                            債務人的信貸違約掉期價；

     •     existing or forecast adverse changes in business, financial or            •    預期將導致債務人履行其債務責
           economic conditions that are expected to cause a significant                   任的能力大幅下降的業務、財務
           decrease in the debtor’s ability to meet its debt obligations;                 或經濟狀況的現有或預測不利變
                                                                                          動；

     •     an actual or expected significant deterioration in the operating          •    債務人經營業績的實際或預期重
           results of the debtor;                                                         大惡化；

     •     an actual or expected significant adverse change in the                   •    導致債務人履行其債務責任的能
           regulatory, economic, or technological environment of the                      力大幅下降的債務人監管、經濟
           debtor that results in a significant decrease in the debtor’s                  或技術環境的實際或預期重大不
           ability to meet its debt obligations.                                          利變動。

     Irrespective of the outcome of the above assessment, the Group                  無論上述評估結果如何，本集團假定合
     presumes that the credit risk has increased significantly since initial         約付款逾期超過30日時，信貸風險自
     recognition when contractual payments are more than 30 days past                初始確認以來已大幅增加，除非本集團
     due, unless the Group has reasonable and supportable information                有合理及可靠資料證明可予收回則當別
     that demonstrates otherwise.                                                    論。

              a financial instrument has not increased significantly since initial                工具被判定為具有較低信貸風險，本集

              risk at the end of each reporting period. A financial instrument is                 認以來並未顯著上升。在以下情況下，
              determined to have low credit risk if (i) it has a low risk of default, (ii)        金融工具會被判定為具有較低信貸風
              the borrower has a strong capacity to meet its contractual cash flow                險：(i)金融工具具有較低違約風險，(ii)

              necessarily, reduce the ability of the borrower to fulfil its contractual           不利變動有可能但未必會降低借款人履
              cash flow obligations. The Group considers a debt instrument to                     行合約現金流義務的能力。當債務工具
              have low credit risk when it has an internal or external credit rating              的內部或外部信貸評級為「投資級別」

                                                                                                  該債務工具信貸風險偏低。

              a party to the irrevocable commitment is considered to be the date                  撤銷承諾訂約方之日期被視為初始確認
              of initial recognition for the purposes of assessing impairment. In                 日期，以評估金融工具的減值。於評估
              assessing whether there has been a significant increase in the                      財務擔保合約之信貸風險自初步確認以
              credit risk since initial recognition of financial guarantee contracts,             來是否顯著增加時，本集團會考慮特定
              the Group considers the changes in the risk that the specified                      債務人將就合約發生違約風險的變動。
              debtor will default on the contract.

              The Group regularly monitors the effectiveness of the criteria used                 本集團定期監察用以識別信貸風險是否
              to identify whether there has been a significant increase in credit                 顯著增加的標準的成效，並於適當時候
              risk and revises them as appropriate to ensure that the criteria are                作出修訂，從而確保有關標準能夠於款
              capable of identifying significant increase in credit risk before the               項逾期前識別信貸風險顯著增加。
              amount becomes past due.

     Definition of default                                                           違約的定義

     For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an event               就內部信貸風險管理而言，當內部產生
     of default occurs when information developed internally or obtained             或獲取自外部來源的資料表明，債務人
     from external sources indicates that the debtor is unlikely to pay all          不太可能根據合約條款支付所有到期款
     amounts due in accordance with the contractual terms.                           項時，本集團認為構成違約事件。

     Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that default has                 無論上述情況如何，倘一項金融資產已
     occurred when a financial asset is more than 90 days past due                   逾期超過90日，則本集團認為已產生
     unless the Group has reasonable and supportable information                     違約，除非本集團有合理及可靠資料證
     to demonstrate that a more lagging default criterion is more                    明較寬鬆的違約標準更為適用則當別
     appropriate.                                                                    論。

     Credit-impaired financial assets                                                已信貸減值金融資產

     A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events that               當一項或多項對金融資產預計未來現金
     have a detrimental impact on the estimated future cash flows of                 流造成負面影響的違約事件發生時，即
     that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that a financial asset             代表金融資產已出現信貸減值。金融資
     is credit-impaired includes observable data about the following                 產出現信貸減值的證據包括涉及以下事
     events:                                                                         件的可觀察數據：

     •     significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;           •     發行方或借款人遇到嚴重財政困
                                                                                           難；

     •     a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;                •     違反合約，如拖欠或逾期的情
                                                                                           況；

     •     the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual                •     與借款人財政困難有關的經濟或
           reasons relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty, having                 合約考慮，給予借款人在其他情
           granted to the borrower a concession(s) that the lender(s)                      況下不會作出的讓步；
           would not otherwise consider;

     •     it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter                      •     借款人有可能破產或進行其他財
           bankruptcy or other financial reorganization.                                   務重組。

              Write-off policy                                                                 撇銷政策

              The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information                 當有資料顯示對手方陷入嚴重財務困
              indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial difficulty and           難，且並無實際收回的可能之時（例如
              there is no realistic prospect of recovery, e.g. when the counterparty           對手方已清算或進入破產程序），本集
              has been placed under liquidation or has entered into bankruptcy                 團會撇銷金融資產。已撇銷的金融資產
              proceedings. Financial assets written off may still be subject to                仍可根據本集團的收回程序實施強制執
              enforcement activities under the Group’s recovery procedures,                    行，在適當情況下考慮法律意見。任何
              taking into account legal advice where appropriate. Any recoveries               收回款項會於損益中確認。
              made are recognized in profit or loss.

              Measurement and recognition of ECL                                               預期信貸虧損的計量及確認

              The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of default,              預期信貸虧損的計量乃指違約概率、違
              loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if there is a default)        約損失率（即倘違約引致損失的程度）及
              and the exposure at default. The assessment of the probability                   達約風險的函數。評估違約概率及違約
              of default and loss given default is based on historical data and                損失率根據過往數據及前瞻性資料而作
              forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased              出。預期信貸虧損的估計反映無偏頗及
              and probability-weighted amount that is determined with the                      概率加權的數額，其乃根據加權的相應
              respective risks of default occurring as the weights. The Group                  違約風險釐定。本集團採用可行權宜方
              uses a practical expedient in estimating ECL on trade receivables                法，利用撥備矩陣估計貿易應收款項的
              using a provision matrix taking into consideration historical credit             預期信貸虧捐，當中計及過往信貸虧損
              loss experience, adjusted for forward looking information that is                經驗，並根據毋須付出不必要努力即可
              available without undue cost and effort.                                         獲得的前職性資料進行調整。

              Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference between all                    一般而言，預期信貸虧損估計為本集團
              contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance                   根據合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本
              with the contract and all the cash flows that the Group expects to               集團預計收取的所有現金流量之間的差
              receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined at initial         額，並按初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼
              recognition.                                                                     現。

     For a financial guarantee contract, the Group is required to make             就財務擔保合約而言，本集團須僅在債
     payments only in the event of a default by the debtor in accordance           務人違反保證文書條款的情況下作出付
     with the terms of the instrument that is guaranteed. Accordingly, the         款。因此，預期信貸虧損是償還持有人
     ECL is the present value of the expected payments to reimburse                信貸損失的預期付款的現值，該信貸損
     the holder for a credit loss that it incurs less any amounts that the         失減去本集團預期從持有人、債務人或
     Group expects to receive from the holder, the debtors or any other            任何其他方獲得的任何金額。
     party.

     For ECL on financial guarantee contracts for which the effective              就無法釐定實際利率的財務擔保合約的
     interest rate cannot be determined, the Group will apply a discount           預期信貸虧損而言，本集團將採納反映
     rate that reflects the current market assessment of the time value of         目前對貨幣時間價值的市場評估及該現
     money and the risks that are specific to the cash flows but only if,          金流特定風險的貼現率，惟僅限於風險
     and to the extent that, the risks are taken into account by adjusting         按調整貼現率而非調整所貼現的現金不
     the discount rate instead of adjusting the cash shortfalls being              足納入考慮時。
     discounted.

     Lifetime ECL for certain trade receivables and other receivables are          貿易應收款項及其他應收款項之存續期
     considered on a collective basis taking into consideration past due           預期信用損失乃經考慮過往逾期資料及
     information and relevant credit information such as forward looking           前瞻性宏觀經濟資料等相關信用資料後
     macroeconomic information.                                                    按整體基準考慮。

     For collective assessment, the Group takes into consideration the             就集體評估而言，本集團於制定組別時
     following characteristics when formulating the grouping:                      考慮以下特徵：

     •     Past-due status;                                                        •     逾期狀況；

     •     Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                               •     債務人的性質、規模及行業；及

     •     External credit ratings where available.                                •     外部信貸評級（倘可獲得）。

     The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to ensure the                管理層定期檢討分組，以確保各組別的
     constituents of each group continue to share similar credit risk              組成部分繼續擁有類似的信貸風險特
     characteristics.                                                              徵。

              Derecognition of financial assets                                                終止確認金融資產

              The Group derecognizes a financial asset only when the rights                    本集團僅在自資產收取現金流量之權利
              to receive cash flows from the assets expire, or when it transfers               屆滿或其將金融資產及擁有有關資產的
              the financial assets and substantially all the risks and rewards                 絕大部分風險及回報轉讓予另一實體時
              of ownership of the assets to another entity. If the Group neither               終止確認金融資產。倘本集團既不轉讓
              transfers nor retains substantially all the risks and rewards of                 亦不保留擁有金融資產的絕大全部風險
              ownership and continues to control the transferred financial asset,              及回報，並繼續控制已轉讓的金融資
              the Group recognizes its retained interest in the asset and an                   產，本集團會就其可能需要支付的金額
              associated liability for amounts it may have to pay. If the Group                確認其於資產及相關負債的保留權益。
              retains substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership of a                倘本集團保留已轉讓金融資產所有權的
              transferred financial asset, the Group continues to recognize the                絕大部分風險及回報，本集團繼續確認
              financial asset and also recognizes a collateralized borrowing for               金融資產，並就已收取之所得款項確認
              the proceeds received.                                                           有抵押借貸。

              On derecognition of a financial asset measured at amortised cost,                於終止確認按攤銷成本計量的金融資產
              the difference between the asset’s carrying amount and the sum of                時，資產的賬面值與已收及應收代價總
              the consideration received and receivable is recognized in profit or             額的差額會在損益中確認。
              loss.

     On derecognition of an investment in a debt instrument classified as               於終止確認分類為按公允值計入其他全
     at FVTOCI, the cumulative gain or loss previously accumulated in                   面收益的債務工具投資時，先前於其他
     the other reserve is reclassified to profit or loss.                               儲備累計之累計收益或虧損乃重新分類

     Estimated impairment of goodwill                                               商譽的估計減值

     Determining whether goodwill is impaired requires an estimation                釐定商譽是否減值需估計獲分配商譽的
     of the recoverable amount of the cash-generating unit (or group of             現金產生單位（或現金產生單位組別）的
     cash-generating units) to which goodwill has been allocated, which             可收回金額，即使用價值或公允值減出
     is the higher of the value in use or fair value less costs of disposal.        售成本的較高者。使用價值計算要求本
     The value in use calculation requires the Group to estimate the                集團估計預期自現金產生單位（或現金
     future cash flows expected to arise from the cash-generating unit              產生單位組別）產生的未來現金流量及
     (or a group of cash-generating units) and a suitable discount rate in          合適貼現率以計算現值。倘實際未來現
     order to calculate the present value. Where the actual future cash             金流量低於預期，或事實及情況變化導
     flows are less than expected, or change in facts and circumstances             致未來現金流量下調或貼現率上調，則
     which results in downward revision of future cash flows or upward              可能出現重大減值虧損。
     revision of discount rate, a material impairment loss may arise.

     is RMB232,435,000 (2022: RMB232,435,000). Details of the                       人民幣232,435,000元（2022年：人民

              During the year, the Group’s revenue represents the amount                    年內，本集團的收益指焦炭及焦化產
              received and receivable from the sales of goods to external                   品、精細化工產品、運營管理服務、貿
              customers arising from the coke and coking chemicals, refined                 易以及銷售物業開發的物業，並向外部
              chemicals, operation management services, trading and sales of                客戶銷售該等產品所收取的金額及應收
              properties arising from property development.                                 款項。

              Revenue from the sales of goods directly to customers is recognized           直接向客戶銷售產品的收益於貨品控制
              when control of the goods has been transferred, being when the                權已轉讓時確認，即產品由客戶於客戶
              products are accepted by the customers at the customer’s specific             的指定地點或本集團廠房接收時。有關
              destination or the Group’s plants. The customers have full discretion         客戶就產品全權控制，並概無可影響客
              over the products, and there is no unfulfilled obligation that could          戶接受產品而尚未達成的責任。本集團
              affect the customers’ acceptance of the products. A receivable                於貨品交付至客戶時確認應收款項，原
              is recognized by the Group when the goods are delivered to the                因是收取代價的權利於這時間點變為無
              customers as this represents the point in time at which the right to          條件，僅須待時過去便可收取付款。所
              consideration becomes unconditional, as only the passage of time is           購買的產品一經接受，客戶無權退回。
              required before payment is due. Customers have no right to return
              the products purchased once accepted.

              Revenue from the management service provided to customers is                  向客戶提供管理服務的收益乃於提供服
              recognized over time when services are provided.                              務時隨時間確認。

              Revenue from sales of properties is recognised at a point in time             銷售物業的收益於竣工物業轉移至客戶
              when the completed property is transferred to customers, being                之時間點確認，即客戶取得竣工物業的
              at the point that the customer obtains the control of the completed           控制權且本集團有即時付款的權利時。
              property and the Group has present right to payment.

              For contracts where the period between payment and transfer of                就相關貨品或服務的支付與轉移期間少
              the associated goods or services is less than one year, the Group             於一年的合約而言，本集團就任何重大
              applies the practical expedient of not adjusting the transaction price        融資成分應用不調整交易價格的實際權
              for any significant financing component.                                      宜方法。

              Principal versus agent                                                        委託人與代理人

              When another party is involved in providing goods or services to a            當另一方涉及向客戶提供商品或服務
              customer, the Group determines whether the nature of its promise is           時，本集團釐定其承諾之性質是否為提
              a performance obligation to provide the specified goods or services           供指定貨品或服務本身之履約責任（即
              itself (i.e. the Group is a principal) or to arrange for those goods          本集團為委託人）或安排由另一方提供
              or services to be provided by the other party (i.e. the Group is an           該等貨品或服務（即本集團為代理人）。
              agent).

              The Group is a principal if it controls the specified good or service         倘本集團在向客戶轉讓貨品或服務之前
              before that good or service is transferred to a customer.                     控制指定貨品或服務，則本集團為委託
                                                                                            人。

     The Group is an agent if its performance obligation is to arrange for          倘本集團之履約責任為安排另一方提供

     this case, the Group does not control the specified good or service            人。在此情況下，在將貨品或服務轉讓

     to the customer. When the Group acts as an agent, it recognizes                的指定貨品或服務。當本集團為代理人

     services to be provided by the other party.                                    佣金之金額確認收益。

37. RESERVES                                                                       37. 儲備

   Share premium                                                                            股份溢價

   Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands,                                 根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），計入本
   amount included in the share premium account of the Company                              公司股份溢價賬中的金額可分派予本公
   is distributable to the shareholders of the Company provided that                        司股東，前提是緊隨建議分派股息日期
   immediately following the date on which the dividend is proposed to                      後本公司將有能力在日常業務過程中償
   be distributed, the Company will be in a position to pay off its debts                   還到期債務。
   as and when they fall due in the ordinary course of business.

   Merger reserve                                                                           合併儲備

   The merger reserve represents the difference between the nominal                         合併儲備指本公司已發行股本面值╱已
   value of the share capital issued/consideration paid by the                              付代價與根據本集團重組所收購附屬公
   Company and the aggregate capital of the subsidiaries acquired                           司合計資本之間的差額，根據合併會計
   pursuant to the group reorganization, accounted for as capital                           基準作為最終控股公司的注資入賬╱分
   contribution from/distribution to ultimate holding company under                         派。
   merger basis of accounting.

       37. RESERVES (CONTINUED)                                                         37. 儲備（續）

              Reserve fund                                                                儲備基金

              The Articles of Association of certain PRC subsidiaries require the         若干中國附屬公司的組織章程細則要求
              appropriation of 10% of their profit after tax each year, based on          每年根據其法定經審核財務報表將其除
              their statutory audited financial statements, to the reserve fund until     稅後溢利的10%撥入儲備基金，直至
              the balance reaches 50% of the registered capital of the respective         結餘達到相應中國附屬公司註冊資本的
              PRC subsidiaries. The reserve fund may be capitalized as the paid-          50%。儲備基金可資本化為該等附屬公
              in capital of these subsidiaries.                                           司的實收資本。

              Safety fund                                                                 安全基金

              Pursuant to relevant PRC regulation, the Group is required to               根據相關中國法規，本集團須將核心業
              transfer 1.5% on revenue generated from the core business into a            務產生的收益1.5%轉入指定基金。該
              designated fund. The fund will be used for installation and repair          基金將用於安全設施的安裝、維修和保
              and maintenance of safety facilities. The movements in the reporting        養。於報告期內變動指根據中國相關法
              periods represent the difference between the amounts provided               規計提的金額與年內使用金額之間的差
              based on the relevant PRC regulation and the amount utilized                額。
              during the year.

              Foreign currency transaction reserve                                        外幣換算儲備

              The foreign currency transaction reserve comprises the foreign              外幣換算儲備包括因人民幣以外的功能
              exchange differences arising from the translation of the financial          貨幣換算海外業務財務報表產生的外匯
              statements of foreign operations with functional currency other than        差額。
              RMB.

              Revaluation reserve                                                         重估儲備

              Revaluation reserve represents the difference between the carrying          重估儲備指投資物業賬面值與其轉移自
              amount of the investment property and its fair value at the date of         物業、廠房及設備當日的公允值之間的
              transfer from property, plant and equipment.                                差額。

              Other reserve                                                               其他儲備

              Other reserve represents the difference between consideration and           其他儲備指與非控股股東進行的股權交
              change of ownership interests in subsidiaries in equity transactions        易中，代價與附屬公司擁有權權益變動
              with non-controlling shareholders.                                          之間的差額。

              During the year ended December 31, 2023, the Group entered                  截至2023年12月31日止年度，本集團
              into an agreement with a third party, pursuant to which the third           與一名第三方訂立協議，據此，第三方

       39. EMPLOYEE RETIREMENT BENEFITS                                              39. 僱員退休福利

              Defined contribution retirement plans                                    界定供款退休計劃

              Pursuant to the relevant labor rules and regulations in the PRC, the     根據中國相關勞工規則及法規，本集團
              PRC subsidiaries of the Group participate in defined contribution        中國附屬公司參與由中國市政府機關經
              retirement benefit schemes (the “Schemes”) organized by the PRC          營的界定供款退休福利計劃（「該等計
              municipal government authorities whereby the Group is required           劃」），據此，本集團須按適用比率向該
              to make contributions to the Schemes based on applicable rates.          等計劃作出供款。市政府機關負責全部
              The municipal government authorities are responsible for the entire      應付已退休僱員的退休金責任。
              pension obligations payable to retired employees.

              The Group also operates a Mandatory Provident Fund Scheme (the            本集團亦根據香港強制性公積金計劃條
              “MPF scheme”) under the Hong Kong Mandatory Provident Fund                例為於香港僱傭條例的司法管轄權下受
              Schemes Ordinance for employees employed under the jurisdiction           聘用且過往並非受界定福利退休計劃涵
              of the Hong Kong Employment Ordinance and not previously                  蓋的僱員，經營一項強制性公積金計劃
              covered by the defined benefit retirement plan. The MPF scheme is        （「強積金計劃」）。強積金計劃乃由獨立
              a defined contribution retirement plan administered by independent        受託人管理的界定供款退休計劃。根據
              trustees. Under the MPF scheme, the employer and its employees            強積金計劃，僱主及其僱員各自須按僱
              are each required to make contributions to the plan at 5% of the          員相關收入的5%向計劃作出供款，每
              employees’ relevant income, subject to a cap of monthly relevant          月相關收入上限為1,500港元。計劃供
              income of HKD1,500. Contributions to the plan vest immediately.           款即時歸屬。

       41. CAPITAL MANAGEMENT                                                         41. 資本管理

              The Group’s primary objectives of managing capital are to                 本集團管理資本的主要目的是維護其持
              safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern, and to      續經營的能力，從而使其能夠透過按合
              provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other stakeholders,     理成本取得融資，繼續為股東提供回報
              by securing access to finance at a reasonable cost.                       及為其他持份者提供利益。

              The Group actively and regularly reviews and manages its capital          本集團積極及定期檢討和管理其資本架
              structure to maintain a balance between the higher shareholder            構，以在維持較高借款水平可能帶來的
              returns that might be possible with higher levels of borrowings, and      高股東回報取得平衡，並因應不同的經
              makes adjustments to the capital structure in light of changes in         濟狀況為資本架構作出調整。
              economic conditions.

              Based on recommendations of the board of directors, the Group will        根據董事會建議，本集團將透過調整向
              balance its overall capital structure through adjusting the amount        股東派付的股息金額、發行新股、籌集
              of dividends paid to shareholders, issuing of new shares, raising         新債務融資或出售資產以減少債務，藉
              new debt financing or selling assets to reduce debt. During the           以平衡其整體資本架構。於報告期內，
              reporting period, there were no changes in the objectives, policies       並無更改目標、政策或程序。
              or processes.

              The Group monitors capital with reference to its debt position. The       本集團透過參考其債務狀況監察資本。
              Group’s strategy was to maintain the equity and debt in a balanced        本集團的策略為將權益與債項維持在一
              position and ensure there was adequate working capital to service         個平衡的位置，並確保擁有足夠的營運
              its debt obligation.                                                      資金償還其債務。

   the Group mainly transacts with state-owned or reputable financial           險，本集團主要與中國國有或聲譽卓著
   institutions in PRC and reputable international financial institutions       的金融機構及中國以外其他地區聲譽卓
   outside of PRC. There has been no recent history of default in               著的國際金融機構交易。該等金融機構
   relation to these financial institutions.                                    近期並無違約記錄。

   To manage risk arising from trade receivable balances, the                   為管理來自貿易應收款項結餘的風險，
   Group has policies in place to ensure that credit terms are                  本集團已制定政策確保向信貸記錄妥當
   made to counterparties with an appropriate credit history and                的交易對手授予信貸期，而管理層會持
   the management performs ongoing credit evaluations of its                    續評估交易對手的信用。授予客戶的信
   counterparties. The credit period granted to the customers and               貸期及此等客戶的信貸質素將就其財務
   the credit quality of these customers is assessed, which takes into          狀況、過往經驗及其他因素進行評估。
   account their financial position, past experience and other factors.

   The Group does not have any significant concentration of credit              本集團並無面對重大集中信貸風險，有
   risk, with exposure spread over a large number of counterparties             關風險分佈於大量交易對手及客戶。
   and customers.

   The Group reassesses lifetime ECL for trade receivables and                  本集團就並無重大融資成分的貿易應收
   amounts due from related parties arising from contracts with                 款項及自客戶合約產生的應收關聯方款
   customers without significant financing component to ensure that             項重估全期預期信貸虧損，以確保就違
   adequate impairment loss are made for significant increase in the            約發生的可能性或風險大幅增加作出足
   likelihood or risk of a default occurring.                                   夠減值虧損。
